# 第 9 章　应用程序

> 本章解决的是系统装好之后该装什么软件的困惑：这里不教安装，而是把散落的应用程序列表整理成按任务分类的索引，先定位包名再交给包管理器。阅读顺序上，最短的《应用程序列表》必读，它讲清清单怎么组织，其他发行版的软件列表和软件 forge 从何而来；接着读《工具》，终端、文件、开发、磁盘、系统都在其中，命中率最高；《多媒体》《文档》按需取用；《安全》《其它》不必通读，真要加固或挑桌面环境时再查。最容易踩的坑是把清单当推荐：条目只是候选，包未必还在官方仓库，有的已停更或改名，功能相近的工具又常常重叠。

<!-- 本章 6 节，共 563 KB -->

## 9.1　应用程序列表

*难度：进阶 ｜ 篇幅：6.4 KB ｜ 所属：应用程序*

将要讲到：通用软件列表、其他发行版的软件列表、软件 forges、专业软件列表、Arch Linux 论坛帖子

>
> ---
>
> [互联网](../../03_网络与服务/网络应用程序/应用程序列表_互联网.md) – [多媒体](应用程序列表_多媒体.md) – [工具](应用程序列表_工具.md) – [文档](应用程序列表_文档.md) – [安全](应用程序列表_安全.md) – [科学](../科学/应用程序列表_科学.md) – [其它](应用程序列表_其它.md) – [GNU](../GNU/GNU.md)

> **翻译状态：**
>
> - 本文（或部分内容）译自 [List of applications](https://wiki.archlinux.org/title/List_of_applications)，最近一次同步于 2025-03-9，若英文版本有所[更改](https://wiki.archlinux.org/title/List_of_applications?diff=0&oldid=828449)，则您可以帮助同步与[翻译](https://wiki.archlinuxcn.org/wzh/Help:%E7%BF%BB%E8%AF%91)更改的内容。
> - 您可以在 [ArchWiki 的对应页面](https://wiki.archlinux.org/title/List_of_applications_(%E7%AE%80%E4%BD%93%E4%B8%AD%E6%96%87)?action=history)找到本文翻译的**原始**修订历史。
> - 本文可能与英文原文存在出入。

本文是一份按类别分类的应用程序总列表，供需要查找软件包的用户参考。许多部分分为控制台和图形应用程序。在“控制台”部分列出的应用程序可能有图形前端，官方前端目前没有列出。

> **提示：**
>
> - 请考虑安装 [pkgstats](../../01_安装与包管理/包管理/Pkgstats.md) 软件包，它提供了一个[定时器](../../02_系统管理与内核/系统管理/Systemd_定时器.md)，可以将您系统上安装的软件包列表以及您使用的体系架构和镜像发送给 Arch Linux 开发人员，以帮助他们确定工作的优先顺序，使发行版变得更好。这些信息是匿名发送的，不会被用来识别你的身份。您可以在[统计页面](https://pkgstats.archlinux.de/packages)查看收集到的数据。更多信息请访问[论坛上的帖子](https://bbs.archlinux.org/viewtopic.php?id=105431)。
> - 守护进程软件包通常会包含相关的 systemd 单元文件，以便启动；有些软件包甚至包含不同的单元文件。安装后，`pacman -Qql *package* | grep -Fe .service -e .socket` 可以用来检查并找到相关文件。

## 9.2　应用程序列表/安全

*难度：高级 ｜ 篇幅：38.4 KB ｜ 所属：应用程序*

有关详细指南，请参阅 ArchWiki 主页，[安全](../../08_安全与加密/安全/安全.md)。

## 网络安全

另见 [Wikipedia:Comparison of packet analyzers](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Comparison_of_packet_analyzers)。

- **airgeddon** — Multi-use bash script to audit wireless networks

: [https://github.com/v1s1t0r1sh3r3/airgeddon](https://github.com/v1s1t0r1sh3r3/airgeddon) || [airgeddon-git](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/airgeddon-git/)AUR

- **[Arpwatch](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arpwatch)** — Tool that monitors ethernet activity and keeps a database of Ethernet/IP address pairings.

: [https://ee.lbl.gov/](https://ee.lbl.gov/) || [arpwatch](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=arpwatch)包

- **bettercap** — Swiss army knife for network attacks and monitoring.

: [https://www.bettercap.org/](https://www.bettercap.org/) || [bettercap](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=bettercap)包

- **darkstat** — Captures network traffic, calculates statistics about usage, and serves reports over HTTP.

: [https://unix4lyfe.org/darkstat/](https://unix4lyfe.org/darkstat/) || [darkstat](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=darkstat)包

- **[dsniff](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/dSniff)** — Collection of tools for network auditing and penetration testing.

: [https://www.monkey.org/~dugsong/dsniff/](https://www.monkey.org/~dugsong/dsniff/) || [dsniff](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=dsniff)包

- **[EtherApe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/EtherApe)** — Graphical network monitor for Unix modeled after etherman. Featuring link layer, IP and TCP modes, it displays network activity graphically. Hosts and links change in size with traffic. Color coded protocols display.

: [https://etherape.sourceforge.io/](https://etherape.sourceforge.io/) || [etherape](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=etherape)包

- **[Ettercap](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ettercap_(software))** — Multipurpose Network sniffer/analyser/interceptor/logger.

: [https://ettercap.github.io/ettercap/](https://ettercap.github.io/ettercap/) || CLI: [ettercap](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=ettercap)包, GUI: [ettercap-gtk](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=ettercap-gtk)包

- **GNOME Network Tools** — GNOME interface for various networking tools.

: [https://gitlab.gnome.org/GNOME/gnome-nettool](https://gitlab.gnome.org/GNOME/gnome-nettool) || [gnome-nettool](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=gnome-nettool)包

- **Honeyd** — Tool that allows the user to set up and run multiple virtual hosts on a computer network.

: [http://www.honeyd.org/](http://www.honeyd.org/) || [honeyd](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/honeyd/)AUR

- **hping** — Command-line oriented TCP/IP packet assembler/analyzer.

: [http://hping.org/](http://hping.org/) || [hping](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=hping)包

- **IPTraf** — Console-based network monitoring utility.

: [https://sourceforge.net/projects/iptraf-ng/](https://sourceforge.net/projects/iptraf-ng/) || [iptraf-ng](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=iptraf-ng)包

- **jnettop** — top-like console network traffic visualizer.

: [https://sourceforge.net/projects/jnettop/](https://sourceforge.net/projects/jnettop/) || [jnettop](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=jnettop)包

- **[justniffer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/justniffer)** — Network protocol analyzer that captures network traffic and produces logs in a customized way, can emulate Apache web server log files, track response times and extract all "intercepted" files from the HTTP traffic.

: [http://justniffer.sourceforge.net/](http://justniffer.sourceforge.net/) || [justniffer](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/justniffer/)AUR

- **Kismet** — 802.11 layer2 wireless network detector, sniffer, and intrusion detection system.

: [https://www.kismetwireless.net/](https://www.kismetwireless.net/) || [kismet](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=kismet)包

- **LinSSID** — Graphical wireless scanner.

: [https://sourceforge.net/projects/linssid/](https://sourceforge.net/projects/linssid/) || [linssid](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=linssid)包

- **Nemesis** — Command-line network packet crafting and injection utility.

: [http://nemesis.sourceforge.net/](http://nemesis.sourceforge.net/) || [nemesis](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/nemesis/)AUR

- **Net Activity Viewer** — Graphical network connections viewer, similar in functionality with Netstat.

: [http://netactview.sourceforge.net/](http://netactview.sourceforge.net/) || [netactview](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/netactview/)AUR

- **[netsniff-ng](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/netsniff-ng)** — High performance Linux network sniffer for packet inspection.

: [http://netsniff-ng.org/](http://netsniff-ng.org/) || [netsniff-ng](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=netsniff-ng)包

- **[ngrep](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ngrep)** — grep-like utility that allows you to search for network packets on an interface.

: [https://github.com/jpr5/ngrep](https://github.com/jpr5/ngrep) || [ngrep](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=ngrep)包

- **Nmap** — Security scanner used to discover hosts and services on a computer network, thus creating a "map" of the network.

: [https://nmap.org/](https://nmap.org/) || CLI: [nmap](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=nmap)包, GUI: [zenmap](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/zenmap/)AUR

- **Ntop** — Network probe that shows network usage in a way similar to what top does for processes.

: [https://www.ntop.org/](https://www.ntop.org/) || [ntop](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/ntop/)AUR

- **pyNeighborhood** — GTK-based SMB/CIFS browsing utility.

: [https://launchpad.net/pyneighborhood](https://launchpad.net/pyneighborhood) || [pyneighborhood](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/pyneighborhood/)AUR

- **Smb4K** — Advanced network neighborhood browser and Samba share mounting utility for KDE.

: [https://smb4k.sourceforge.io/](https://smb4k.sourceforge.io/) || [smb4k](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=smb4k)包

- **Snort** — Network intrusion prevention and detection system.

: [https://www.snort.org/](https://www.snort.org/) || [snort](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/snort/)AUR

- **Spectools** — A set of utilities for spectrum analyzer hardware including Wi-Spy devices.

: [https://www.kismetwireless.net/static/spectools/](https://www.kismetwireless.net/static/spectools/) || [spectools](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/spectools/)AUR

- **[Sshguard](../../03_网络与服务/防火墙/Sshguard.md)** — Daemon that protects SSH and other services against brute-force attacks, similar to Fail2ban.

: [https://www.sshguard.net/](https://www.sshguard.net/) || [sshguard](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=sshguard)包

- **Suricata** — High performance Network IDS, IPS and Network Security Monitoring engine.

: [https://suricata-ids.org/](https://suricata-ids.org/)\[[失效链接](https://zh.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:%E5%A4%B1%E6%95%88%E9%93%BE%E6%8E%A5) 2022-09-20 ⓘ\] || [suricata](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/suricata/)AUR

- **Tcpdump** — Common console-based packet analyzer that allows the user to intercept and display TCP/IP and other packets being transmitted or received over a network.

: [https://www.tcpdump.org/](https://www.tcpdump.org/) || [tcpdump](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=tcpdump)包

- **[vnStat](../../03_网络与服务/网络/VnStat.md)** — Console-based network traffic monitor that keeps a log of network traffic for the selected interfaces.

: [https://humdi.net/vnstat/](https://humdi.net/vnstat/) || [vnstat](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=vnstat)包

- **What IP** — Small GTK application to get info on your IP.

: [https://gabmus.gitlab.io/whatip/](https://gabmus.gitlab.io/whatip/) || [whatip](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/whatip/)AUR

- **[Wireshark](../../08_安全与加密/安全/Wireshark.md)** — Network protocol analyzer that lets you capture and interactively browse the traffic running on a computer network.

: [https://www.wireshark.org/](https://www.wireshark.org/) || CLI: [wireshark-cli](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=wireshark-cli)包, GUI: [wireshark-qt](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=wireshark-qt)包

- **[Xplico](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Xplico)** — Network forensics analysis tool (NFAT), which is a software that reconstructs the contents of acquisitions performed with a packet sniffer.

: [https://www.xplico.org/](https://www.xplico.org/) || [xplico](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/xplico/)AUR

- **Zeek** — Powerful network analysis framework that is much different from the typical IDS you may know.

: [https://zeek.org/](https://zeek.org/) || [zeek](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/zeek/)AUR

## 威胁和漏洞检测

- **AFICK** — Security tool that allows to monitor the changes on your file systems, and so can detect intrusions.

: [http://afick.sourceforge.net/](http://afick.sourceforge.net/) || [afick](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/afick/)AUR

- **[Lynis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lynis)** — Security and system auditing tool to harden Unix/Linux systems.

: [https://cisofy.com/lynis/](https://cisofy.com/lynis/) || [lynis](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=lynis)包

- **Metasploit Framework** — An advanced open-source platform for developing, testing, and using exploit code.

: [https://www.metasploit.com/](https://www.metasploit.com/) || [metasploit](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=metasploit)包

- **Nikto** — Nikto is an Open Source (GPL) web server scanner.

: [https://github.com/sullo/nikto](https://github.com/sullo/nikto) || [nikto](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=nikto)包

- **Nessus** — Comprehensive vulnerability scanning program.

: [https://www.tenable.com/products/nessus](https://www.tenable.com/products/nessus) || [nessus](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/nessus/)AUR

- **OpenVAS** — Framework of several services and tools offering a comprehensive and powerful vulnerability scanning and vulnerability management solution. FOSS Nessus fork.

: [https://www.openvas.org/](https://www.openvas.org/) || [openvas-scanner](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/openvas-scanner/)AUR

- **OSSEC** — Open Source Host-based Intrusion Detection System that performs log analysis, file integrity checking, policy monitoring, rootkit detection, real-time alerting and active response.

: [https://ossec.github.io/](https://ossec.github.io/) || [ossec-local](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/ossec-local/)AUR, [ossec-server](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/ossec-server/)AUR

- **Samhain** — Host-based intrusion detection system (HIDS) provides file integrity checking and log file monitoring/analysis, as well as rootkit detection, port monitoring, detection of rogue SUID executables, and hidden processes.

: [https://www.la-samhna.de/samhain/index.html](https://www.la-samhna.de/samhain/index.html) || [samhain-client](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/samhain-client/)AUR, [samhain-server](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/samhain-server/)AUR, [samhain-standalone](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/samhain-standalone/)AUR

- **[Tiger](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tiger_(security_software))** — Security tool that can be used both as a security audit and intrusion detection system.

: [https://www.nongnu.org/tiger/](https://www.nongnu.org/tiger/) || [tiger](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/tiger/)AUR

- **[Tripwire](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Open_Source_Tripwire)** — Intrusion detection system.

: [https://github.com/Tripwire/tripwire-open-source](https://github.com/Tripwire/tripwire-open-source) || [tripwire-git](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/tripwire-git/)AUR

## 屏幕锁定器

> [![](https://wiki.archlinuxcn.org/wzh/images/c/c9/Merge-arrows-2.png)](https://wiki.archlinuxcn.org/wzh/File:Merge-arrows-2.png)**本文或本章节可能需要合并到Session lock#By environment。**
>
> **附注：** Same purpose.（在 [Talk:应用程序列表/安全](https://wiki.archlinuxcn.org/wiki/Talk:%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E7%A8%8B%E5%BA%8F%E5%88%97%E8%A1%A8/%E5%AE%89%E5%85%A8) 中讨论）

另见会话锁（英语：[Session lock](https://wiki.archlinux.org/title/Session_lock)）。

> **警告：**仅 *sflock*, *physlock*, *Cinnamon Screensaver*, *MATE Screensaver* 和 *GNOME Screensaver* 能够阻止 tty 访问。参见 [Xorg#阻止 TTY 访问](../../05_桌面与图形/X%20服务器/Xorg.md#阻止_TTY_访问)获得如何手动阻止 tty 访问的信息。

- **betterlockscreen** — *i3lock-color* wrapper. Betterlockscreen allows you to cache images with different filters and lockscreen with blazing speed.

: [https://github.com/pavanjadhaw/betterlockscreen](https://github.com/pavanjadhaw/betterlockscreen) || [betterlockscreen](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/betterlockscreen/)AUR

- **Cinnamon Screensaver** — Screen locker for the Cinnamon desktop.

: [https://github.com/linuxmint/cinnamon-screensaver](https://github.com/linuxmint/cinnamon-screensaver) || [cinnamon-screensaver](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=cinnamon-screensaver)包

- **Deepin Screensaver** — A lightweight Qt5 based screensaver.

: [https://github.com/linuxdeepin/deepin-screensaver](https://github.com/linuxdeepin/deepin-screensaver) || [deepin-screensaver](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=deepin-screensaver)包

- **GNOME Screensaver** — Legacy screen locker for the GNOME desktop. Unmaintained since 2012.

: [https://wiki.gnome.org/Attic/GnomeScreensaver](https://wiki.gnome.org/Attic/GnomeScreensaver) || [gnome-screensaver](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/gnome-screensaver/)AUR

- **i3lock** — A simple screen locker. Provides user feedback and uses PAM authentication. The background can be set to an image or solid color.

: [https://i3wm.org/i3lock/](https://i3wm.org/i3lock/) || [i3lock](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=i3lock)包

- **i3lock-blur** — Fork of *i3lock* which can use your desktop with the blur effect applied as a background.

: [https://github.com/karulont/i3lock-blur](https://github.com/karulont/i3lock-blur) || [i3lock-blur](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/i3lock-blur/)AUR

- **i3lock-color** — Fork of *i3lock* with color and positioning configuration support and can use your desktop with the blur effect applied as a background.

: [https://github.com/Raymo111/i3lock-color](https://github.com/Raymo111/i3lock-color) || [i3lock-color](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/i3lock-color/)AUR

- **Light-locker** — A simple locker (forked from *gnome-screensaver*) that aims to have simple, sane, secure defaults and be well integrated with the desktop while not carrying any desktop-specific dependencies. It relies on [LightDM](../Canonical/LightDM.md) for locking and unlocking your session via ConsoleKit/UPower or *logind/systemd*.

: [https://github.com/the-cavalry/light-locker](https://github.com/the-cavalry/light-locker) || [light-locker](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=light-locker)包

- **MATE Screensaver** — Screensaver and locker for MATE Desktop Environment.

: [https://github.com/mate-desktop/mate-screensaver](https://github.com/mate-desktop/mate-screensaver) || [mate-screensaver](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=mate-screensaver)包

- **physlock** — Screen and console locker.

: [https://github.com/muennich/physlock](https://github.com/muennich/physlock) || [physlock](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=physlock)包

- **sflock** — Simple screen locker utility for X, based on slock. Provides a very basic user feedback.

: [https://github.com/benruijl/sflock](https://github.com/benruijl/sflock) || [sflock-git](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/sflock-git/)AUR

- **[slock](../../05_桌面与图形/Suckless/Slock.md)** — Very simple and lightweight X screen locker. Offers only a black background when locked, there are no animations or text fields.

: [https://tools.suckless.org/slock/](https://tools.suckless.org/slock/) || [slock](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=slock)包

- **sxlock** — Fork of sflock with a few enhancements. Provides basic user feedback, uses PAM authentication, supports DPMS and RandR. Supports `sxlock.service` to lock the screen on suspend/hibernation. See the [README](https://github.com/lahwaacz/sxlock/blob/master/README.md) for more information.

: [https://github.com/lahwaacz/sxlock](https://github.com/lahwaacz/sxlock) || [sxlock-git](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/sxlock-git/)AUR

- **tsscreenlock** — Screen locker used in theShell. Shows music controls, and if used with theShell, also shows desktop notifications.

: [https://github.com/vicr123/tsscreenlock](https://github.com/vicr123/tsscreenlock) || [tsscreenlock](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/tsscreenlock/)AUR

- **vlock** — TTY locker. A mirror of the [original vlock](https://lists.archlinux.org/archives/list/aur-general@lists.archlinux.org/message/UUI4XXDA5RMOTYOQJUYCES33LE4VY2U5/) is available at [github](https://github.com/WorMzy/vlock).

: [https://kbd-project.org/](https://kbd-project.org/) || [kbd](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=kbd)包

- **xfce4-screensaver** — A screen saver and locker that aims to have simple, sane, secure defaults and be well integrated with the xfce desktop.

: [https://git.xfce.org/apps/xfce4-screensaver/about/](https://git.xfce.org/apps/xfce4-screensaver/about/) || [xfce4-screensaver](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=xfce4-screensaver)包

- **xlockmore** — Simple X11 screen lock with PAM support.

: [https://sillycycle.com/xlockmore.html](https://sillycycle.com/xlockmore.html) || [xlockmore](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=xlockmore)包

- **[XScreenSaver](../../05_桌面与图形/X%20服务器/XScreenSaver.md)** — Screen saver and locker for the X Window System.

: [https://www.jwz.org/xscreensaver/](https://www.jwz.org/xscreensaver/) || [xscreensaver](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=xscreensaver)包

- **XSecureLock** — X11 screen lock utility designed with the primary goal of security.

: [https://github.com/google/xsecurelock](https://github.com/google/xsecurelock) || [xsecurelock](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=xsecurelock)包

- **xtrlock** — Very lightweight X display locker. Keeps windows visible and displays lock icon instead of mouse cursor. Typing password followed by enter unlocks the screen.

: [https://packages.debian.org/sid/xtrlock](https://packages.debian.org/sid/xtrlock) || [xtrlock](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=xtrlock)包

## 密码学

### 哈希校验器

- **cfv** — Tiny utility to both test and create checksum files, support *.sfv*, *.csv*, *.crc*, *.md5*, *md5sum*, *sha1sum*, *.torrent*, *par*, and *.par2* files.

: [http://cfv.sourceforge.net/](http://cfv.sourceforge.net/) || [cfv](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/cfv/)AUR

- **GtkHash** — A GTK utility for computing message digests or checksums

: [https://github.com/tristanheaven/gtkhash](https://github.com/tristanheaven/gtkhash) || [gtkhash](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/gtkhash/)AUR

- **hashdeep** — A cross-platform tools to compute hashes, or message digests, for any number of files

: [https://md5deep.sourceforge.net/](https://md5deep.sourceforge.net/) || [hashdeep](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=hashdeep)包

- **Quick Hash GUI** — A GUI to enable the rapid selection and subsequent hashing of files (individually or recursively throughout a folder structure) text and (on Linux) disks.

: [https://www.quickhash-gui.org/](https://www.quickhash-gui.org/) || [quickhash-gui-bin](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/quickhash-gui-bin/)AUR

- **RHash** — Utility for verifying hash sums (SFV, CRC, etc). Supports lots of algorithms.

: [https://github.com/rhash/RHash/](https://github.com/rhash/RHash/) || [rhash](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=rhash)包

- **MassHash** — A set of file hashing tools (both CLI and GTK GUI) written in Python. Supported algorithms include MD5, SHA-1, SHA-224, SHA-256, SHA-384, SHA-512.

: [https://jdleicher.github.io/MassHash/](https://jdleicher.github.io/MassHash/) || [masshash](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/masshash/)AUR

- **Parchive** — Utility which creates and uses PAR2 files to detect damage in data files and repair them if necessary.

: [https://github.com/Parchive/par2cmdline](https://github.com/Parchive/par2cmdline) || [par2cmdline](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=par2cmdline)包

### 加密，签名，隐写

- **Argon2** — Argon2 is a password-hashing function that summarizes the state of the art in the design of memory-hard functions and can be used to hash passwords for credential storage, key derivation, or other applications.

: [https://github.com/P-H-C/phc-winner-argon2](https://github.com/P-H-C/phc-winner-argon2) || [argon2](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=argon2)包

- **age** — A simple, modern and secure encryption tool (and library) with small explicit keys, no config options, and UNIX-style composability.

### Personal information managers

: [https://kontact.kde.org/](https://kontact.kde.org/) || [kontact](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=kontact)包

### System tray

Desktop environments typically have their own system tray implementation. E.g. [KDE](../../05_桌面与图形/KDE/KDE.md) ships with Plasma Panel and [Xfce](../../05_桌面与图形/Xfce/Xfce.md) ships with [xfce4-panel](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=xfce4-panel)包. For [GNOME](../../05_桌面与图形/GNOME/GNOME.md), see [GNOME#AppIndicators/Top_bar_icons](../../05_桌面与图形/GNOME/GNOME.md#AppIndicators/Top_bar_icons). For [dwm](../../05_桌面与图形/动态窗口管理器/Dwm.md), see [systray patch](https://dwm.suckless.org/patches/systray/).

- **AllTray** — Dock other applications into the system tray (notification area).

: [https://github.com/mbt/alltray](https://github.com/mbt/alltray) || [alltray](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=alltray)包

- **Docker** — Docking application which acts as a system tray.

: [https://icculus.org/openbox/2/docker/](https://icculus.org/openbox/2/docker/) || [docker-tray](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/docker-tray/)AUR

- **KDocker** — Dock any application in the system tray (notification area).

: [https://github.com/user-none/KDocker](https://github.com/user-none/KDocker) || [kdocker](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/kdocker/)AUR

- **Stalonetray** — Stand-alone freedesktop.org and KDE system tray (notification area) for [Xorg](../../05_桌面与图形/X%20服务器/Xorg.md). It has full XEMBED support and minimal dependencies: an X11 lib only. Stalonetray works with virtually any EWMH-compliant window manager.

: [https://stalonetray.sourceforge.net/](https://stalonetray.sourceforge.net/) || [stalonetray](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=stalonetray)包

- **Trayer** — Lightweight GTK-based system tray (notification area).

: [https://github.com/sargon/trayer-srg/](https://github.com/sargon/trayer-srg/) || [trayer](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=trayer)包

#### Tray indicators

Desktop-independent tray indicators. Useful for window managers without built-in tray widgets:

- [cbatticon](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=cbatticon)包: battery
- [volumeicon](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=volumeicon)包: volume
- [pasystray](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=pasystray)包: pulseaudio
- [sbxkb](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=sbxkb)包: keyboard layout
- [python-pystray](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/python-pystray/)AUR: Python library for creating tray entries.
- [tktray](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/tktray/)AUR: Tk extension to create system tray icons following XDG specifications.

## 文档与文本

### 光学字符识别（OCR）软件

#### 控制台

参见 [Wikipedia:Comparison of optical character recognition software](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Comparison_of_optical_character_recognition_software)。

- **[CuneiForm](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/CuneiForm_(software))** — Command line OCR system originally developed and open sourced by Cognitive technologies. Supported languages: eng, ger, fra, rus, swe, spa, ita, ruseng, ukr, srp, hrv, pol, dan, por, dut, cze, rum, hun, bul, slo, lav, lit, est, tur.

: [https://launchpad.net/cuneiform-linux](https://launchpad.net/cuneiform-linux) || [cuneiform](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=cuneiform)包

- **[GOCR](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/GOCR)** — OCR engine which also supports barcode recognition.

: [https://www-e.uni-magdeburg.de/jschulen/ocr/](https://www-e.uni-magdeburg.de/jschulen/ocr/) || [gocr](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=gocr)包

- **[Ocrad](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ocrad)** — OCR program based on a feature extraction method.

: [https://www.gnu.org/software/ocrad/](https://www.gnu.org/software/ocrad/) || [ocrad](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=ocrad)包

- **OCRmyPDF** — Adds an OCR text layer to scanned PDF files, allowing them to be searched.

: [https://github.com/jbarlow83/OCRmyPDF](https://github.com/jbarlow83/OCRmyPDF) || [ocrmypdf](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/ocrmypdf/)AUR

- **[OCRopus](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/OCRopus)** — OCR *platform*, modules exist for document layout analysis, OCR engines (it can use Tesseract or its own engine), natural language modeling, etc.

: [https://github.com/tmbdev/ocropy](https://github.com/tmbdev/ocropy) || [ocropy-git](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/ocropy-git/)AUR

- **[Tesseract](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tesseract_(software))** — Accurate open source OCR engine. Package splitted, you need install some datafiles for each language ([tesseract-data-eng](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=tesseract-data-eng)包 for example).

: [https://github.com/tesseract-ocr](https://github.com/tesseract-ocr) || [tesseract](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=tesseract)包

#### 图形界面

- **gImageReader** — Graphical GTK/Qt frontend to Tesseract.

: [https://github.com/manisandro/gImageReader](https://github.com/manisandro/gImageReader) || GTK: [gimagereader-gtk](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=gimagereader-gtk)包, Qt: [gimagereader-qt](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=gimagereader-qt)包

- **[gscan2pdf](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Scanner_Access_Now_Easy#gscan2pdf)** — Scans, runs an OCR engine, minor post-processing, creates a document.

: [https://gscan2pdf.sourceforge.net/](https://gscan2pdf.sourceforge.net/) || [gscan2pdf](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=gscan2pdf)包

- **Linux-Intelligent-Ocr-Solution** — Easy-OCR solution and Tesseract trainer for converting print into text using either scanner or a camera.

: [https://sourceforge.net/projects/lios/](https://sourceforge.net/projects/lios/) || [lios-git](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/lios-git/)AUR

- **[OCRFeeder](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/OCRFeeder)** — Python GUI for Gnome which performs document analysis and rendition, and can use either CuneiForm, GOCR, Ocrad or Tesseract as OCR engines. It can import from PDF or image files, and export to HTML or OpenDocument.

: [https://wiki.gnome.org/Apps/OCRFeeder](https://wiki.gnome.org/Apps/OCRFeeder) || [ocrfeeder](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=ocrfeeder)包

- **Paperwork** — Personal document manager. It manages scanned documents and PDFs.

: [https://openpaper.work/](https://openpaper.work/) || [paperwork](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=paperwork)包

- **Scans to PDF** — Create small, searchable PDFs from scanned documents.

: [https://github.com/Unrud/djpdf](https://github.com/Unrud/djpdf) || [djpdf](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/djpdf/)AUR

- **YAGF** — Graphical interface for the CuneiForm text recognition program on the Linux platform.

: [https://sourceforge.net/projects/yagf-ocr/](https://sourceforge.net/projects/yagf-ocr/) || [yagf](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/yagf/)AUR

## 9.5　应用程序列表/工具

## 终端

### 命令行解释器

主要内容参看主文章：[命令行解释器](../../07_开发与工具/命令行解释器/命令行解释器.md)。 参考资料 [Wikipedia:Comparison of command shells](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Comparison_of_command_shells).

### 终端模拟器

终端模拟器显示一个包含终端的 GUI 窗口。大多数模拟 Xterm，而 Xterm 又模拟 VT102，VT102模拟打字机。欲了解更多背景信息，参见 [Wikipedia:Terminal emulator](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Terminal_emulator)。 有关完整列表，参见 [Wikipedia:List of terminal emulators](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_terminal_emulators)。

#### 基于 KMS

以下终端模拟器基于[内核级显示模式设置](../../02_系统管理与内核/内核/内核级显示模式设置.md)，可以在没有 X 的情况下调用。

- **[KMSCON](../../07_开发与工具/终端模拟器/KMSCON.md)** — A KMS/DRM-based system console(getty) with an integrated terminal emulator for Linux operating systems.

: [https://github.com/kmscon/kmscon](https://github.com/kmscon/kmscon) || [kmscon](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=kmscon)包

#### 基于 framebuffer

在 GNU/Linux 世界中，[framebuffer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Framebuffer) 可以引用 Linux 内核中的虚拟设备（**fbdev**）或 X 的虚拟 framebuffer 系统（**xvfb**）。本节主要列出基于内核虚拟设备(即 **fbdev**)的终端模拟器。

- **[Fbterm](../../07_开发与工具/终端模拟器/Fbterm.md)** — a fast FrameBuffer based TERMinal emulator for linux

: [https://salsa.debian.org/debian/fbterm](https://salsa.debian.org/debian/fbterm) || [fbterm](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/fbterm/)AUR

- **yaft** — A simple terminal emulator for living without X, with UCS2 glyphs, wallpaper and 256color support.

: [https://github.com/uobikiemukot/yaft](https://github.com/uobikiemukot/yaft) || [yaft](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/yaft/)AUR

另见 [Wikipedia:Terminal pager](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Terminal_pager)。

: [https://github.com/PaulJuliusMartinez/jless](https://github.com/PaulJuliusMartinez/jless) || [jless](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=jless)包

- **[less](../../07_开发与工具/命令行/核心工具.md)** — A program similar to more, but with support for both forward and backward scrolling, as well as partial loading of files.

: [https://www.gnu.org/software/less/](https://www.gnu.org/software/less/) || [less](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=less)包

- **mcview** — A pager with mouse and colour support. It is bundled with midnight commander.

: [https://midnight-commander.org/](https://midnight-commander.org/) || [mc](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=mc)包

- **moor** — Moor is a pager. It's designed to just do the right thing without any configuration.

: [https://github.com/walles/moor](https://github.com/walles/moor) || [moor](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=moor)包

- **[more](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/More_(command))** — A simple and feature-light pager. It is a part of util-linux.

: [https://git.kernel.org/pub/scm/utils/util-linux/util-linux.git/about/](https://git.kernel.org/pub/scm/utils/util-linux/util-linux.git/about/) || [util-linux](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=util-linux)包

- **[most](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Most_(Unix))** — A pager with support for multiple windows, left and right scrolling, and built-in colour support

: [https://www.jedsoft.org/most/](https://www.jedsoft.org/most/) || [most](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=most)包

- **ov** — Feature-rich terminal-based text viewer. It is a so-called terminal pager.

: [https://noborus.github.io/ov/](https://noborus.github.io/ov/) || [ov-bin](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/ov-bin/)AUR

> **提示：**[Vim](../../07_开发与工具/文本编辑器/Vim.md#Vim_as_a_pager) 和 [Neovim](../../07_开发与工具/文本编辑器/Neovim.md#Use_as_a_pager) 也可以用作终端翻页器。

- **[byobu](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Byobu_(software))** — A GPLv3 licensed addon for tmux or screen. It requires a terminal multiplexer installed.

: [https://byobu.org/](https://byobu.org/) || [byobu](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/byobu/)AUR

- **dtach** — Program that emulates the detach feature of [GNU Screen](../GNU/GNU%20Screen.md).

: [https://dtach.sourceforge.net/](https://dtach.sourceforge.net/) || [dtach](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/dtach/)AUR

- **dvtm** — [dwm](../../05_桌面与图形/动态窗口管理器/Dwm.md)\-style window manager in the console.

: [https://www.brain-dump.org/projects/dvtm/](https://www.brain-dump.org/projects/dvtm/) || [dvtm](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=dvtm)包

- **[GNU Screen](../GNU/GNU%20Screen.md)** — Full-screen window manager that multiplexes a physical terminal.

: [https://www.gnu.org/software/screen/](https://www.gnu.org/software/screen/) || [screen](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=screen)包

- **mtm** — Simple terminal multiplexer with just four commands: change focus, split, close, and screen redraw.

: [https://github.com/deadpixi/mtm](https://github.com/deadpixi/mtm) || [mtm-git](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/mtm-git/)AUR

- **tmate** — A fork of [tmux](../../07_开发与工具/终端多路复用器/Tmux.md) aiming to help with remote access and pair programming.

: [https://tmate.io/](https://tmate.io/) || [tmate](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=tmate)包

- **[tmux](../../07_开发与工具/终端多路复用器/Tmux.md)** — BSD licensed terminal multiplexer.

: [https://tmux.github.io/](https://tmux.github.io/) || [tmux](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=tmux)包

- **zellij** — Terminal multiplexer on steroids written in Rust

: [https://zellij.dev/](https://zellij.dev/) || [zellij](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=zellij)包

> **提示：**[Kitty](../../07_开发与工具/终端多路复用器/Kitty.md) 和 *WezTerm* 是同样提供类似功能的[终端模拟器](#终端模拟器)。

- **Worker** — Fast, lightweight and feature-rich file manager for the X Window System.

: [http://www.boomerangsworld.de/cms/worker/](http://www.boomerangsworld.de/cms/worker/) || [worker](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/worker/)AUR

- **[Xfe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Xfe)** — Microsoft Explorer-like file manager for X (X File Explorer).

: [http://roland65.free.fr/xfe/](http://roland65.free.fr/xfe/) || [xfe](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/xfe/)AUR

### 文件同步和备份

> [![](https://wiki.archlinuxcn.org/wzh/images/c/c9/Merge-arrows-2.png)](https://wiki.archlinuxcn.org/wzh/File:Merge-arrows-2.png)**本文或本章节可能需要合并到[同步和备份程序#数据同步](../../02_系统管理与内核/同步/同步和备份程序.md#数据同步)。**
>
> **附注：** 存在专门的页面。（在 [Talk:应用程序列表/工具](https://wiki.archlinuxcn.org/wiki/Talk:%E5%BA%94%E7%94%A8%E7%A8%8B%E5%BA%8F%E5%88%97%E8%A1%A8/%E5%B7%A5%E5%85%B7) 中讨论）

另见[同步和备份程序](../../02_系统管理与内核/同步/同步和备份程序.md)、[Wikipedia:Comparison of file synchronization software](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Comparison_of_file_synchronization_software) 和 [Wikipedia:Comparison of backup software](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Comparison_of_backup_software)。

#### 控制台

- **Better ADB Sync** — An [rsync](../../02_系统管理与内核/同步/Rsync.md)\-like program to synchronize files between a computer and an Android device using the [Android Debug Bridge](../../04_硬件与外设/Android/Android%20调试桥.md) protocol.

: [https://github.com/jpstotz/better-adb-sync](https://github.com/jpstotz/better-adb-sync) || [better-adb-sync-git](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/better-adb-sync-git/)AUR

- **Borg** — Deduplicating backup program with compression and authenticated encryption that supports backing up over ssh.

: [https://www.borgbackup.org](https://www.borgbackup.org) || [borg](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=borg)包

- **bup** — Efficient backup system based on the git packfile format, providing fast incremental saves and global deduplication.

: [https://bup.github.io/](https://bup.github.io/) || [bup](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=bup)包

- **duplicity** — Simple command-line utility which allows encrypted compressed incremental backup to nearly any storage.

: [https://www.nongnu.org/duplicity/](https://www.nongnu.org/duplicity/) || [duplicity](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=duplicity)包

- **Duply** — Command-line front-end for duplicity which simplifies running it. It manages backup job settings in profiles and allows to batch execute commands.

: [https://www.duply.net/](https://www.duply.net/) || [duply](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/duply/)AUR

- **[git-annex](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/git-annex)** — Manage files with git, without checking the file contents into git.

: [https://git-annex.branchable.com/](https://git-annex.branchable.com/) || [git-annex](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=git-annex)包

- **hsync** — Command line program to sync only those files that have been renamed/moved but otherwise unchanged. It works by issuing simple move operations at the destination without actually transferring the files, and is meant to be used in conjunction with other synchronization programs that lack this capability.

: [https://gitlab.com/ambrevar/hsync](https://gitlab.com/ambrevar/hsync) || [hsync](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/hsync/)AUR

- **Kopia** — Cross-platform backup tool with fast, incremental backups, client-side end-to-end encryption, compression and data deduplication.

: [https://kopia.io/](https://kopia.io/) || [kopia](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/kopia/)AUR

- **rclone** — Command line program to sync files and directories to and from Amazon S3, Dropbox, Google Drive, Microsoft OneDrive, Yandex Disk and many other cloud storage services as well as between local paths.

: [https://rclone.org/](https://rclone.org/) || [rclone](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=rclone)包

- **restic** — Fast, secure, efficient backup program that supports backing up to many cloud services.

: [https://restic.net/](https://restic.net/) || [restic](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=restic)包

- **[rsync](../../02_系统管理与内核/同步/Rsync.md)** — File transfer program that uses the "rsync algorithm" which provides a very fast method for bringing remote files into sync. It does this by sending just the differences in the files across the link, without requiring that both sets of files are present at one of the ends of the link beforehand. Has [multiple frontends available](../../02_系统管理与内核/同步/Rsync.md#Front-ends).

: [https://rsync.samba.org/](https://rsync.samba.org/) || [rsync](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=rsync)包

- **[Tarsnap](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tarsnap)** — Secure, efficient proprietary online backup service.

: [https://www.tarsnap.com/](https://www.tarsnap.com/) || [tarsnap](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=tarsnap)包

- **[Unison](../../03_网络与服务/互联网应用/Unison.md)** — File synchronization tool that allows two replicas of a collection of files and directories to be stored on different hosts (or different disks on the same host), modified separately, and then brought up to date by propagating the changes in each replica to the other.

: [https://github.com/bcpierce00/unison](https://github.com/bcpierce00/unison) || [unison](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=unison)包

- **YARsync** — A Python wrapper around rsync, providing file synchronization and backup with an interface similar to git.

: [https://github.com/ynikitenko/yarsync](https://github.com/ynikitenko/yarsync) || [yarsync](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/yarsync/)AUR

#### 图形界面

- **Déjà Dup** — Simple GTK backup program. It hides the complexity of doing backups the 'right way' (encrypted, off-site, and regular) and uses Restic as the backend.

: [https://apps.gnome.org/DejaDup/](https://apps.gnome.org/DejaDup/) || [deja-dup](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=deja-dup)包

- **[Duplicati](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Duplicati)** — Backup client that securely stores encrypted, incremental, compressed backups on cloud storage services and remote file servers.

: [https://www.duplicati.com/](https://www.duplicati.com/) || [duplicati-canary-bin](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/duplicati-canary-bin/)AUR

- **[FreeFileSync](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FreeFileSync)** — Folder comparison and synchronization software that creates and manages backup copies of all your important files.

: [https://www.freefilesync.org/](https://www.freefilesync.org/) || [freefilesync](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/freefilesync/)AUR

- **[grsync](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/grsync)** — GTK GUI for rsync to synchronize folders, files and make backups

: [https://www.opbyte.it/grsync/](https://www.opbyte.it/grsync/) || [grsync](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=grsync)包

- **KBackup** — Backup program with an easy to use user interface based on the KDE Frameworks. Part of [kde-utilities](https://archlinux.org/groups/x86_64/kde-utilities/)包组.

: [https://apps.kde.org/kbackup/](https://apps.kde.org/kbackup/) || [kbackup](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=kbackup)包

- **Kopia UI** — [Electron](../../07_开发与工具/框架/Electron.md)\-based GUI for Kopia.

: [https://kopia.io/](https://kopia.io/) || [kopia-ui-bin](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/kopia-ui-bin/)AUR

- **Kup** — Backup scheduler for the Plasma desktop. It uses [bup](https://bup.github.io/) and [rsync](../../02_系统管理与内核/同步/Rsync.md) as backend.

: [https://apps.kde.org/kup/](https://apps.kde.org/kup/) || [kup](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=kup)包

- **Ludusavi** — Tool for backing up your PC video game save data, written in Rust. It is cross-platform and supports multiple game stores.

: [https://github.com/mtkennerly/ludusavi/](https://github.com/mtkennerly/ludusavi/) || [ludusavi](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/ludusavi/)AUR

- **Pika Backup** — Easy-to-use backup tool to keep your data safe. Uses Borg as backend.

: [https://apps.gnome.org/PikaBackup/](https://apps.gnome.org/PikaBackup/) || [pika-backup](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=pika-backup)包

- **Save Desktop** — Save your Linux desktop environment configuration.

: [https://vikdevelop.github.io/SaveDesktop/](https://vikdevelop.github.io/SaveDesktop/) || [savedesktop](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/savedesktop/)AUR

- **[SparkleShare](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SparkleShare)** — File sharing and collaboration application written in C#. It can sync with any Git server over SSH.

: [https://www.sparkleshare.org/](https://www.sparkleshare.org/) || [sparkleshare](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/sparkleshare/)AUR

- **StepSync** — File and folder synchronization tool for GNUstep.

: [https://www.nongnu.org/gap/stepsync/](https://www.nongnu.org/gap/stepsync/) || [stepsync.app](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/stepsync.app/)AUR

- **Syncany** — Cloud storage and filesharing application with a focus on security and abstraction of storage.

: [https://www.syncany.org/](https://www.syncany.org/) || [syncany](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/syncany/)AUR

- **Syncthing** — Continuous file synchronization program. It synchronizes files between two or more computers in a simple way without advanced configuration.

: [https://syncthing.net/](https://syncthing.net/) || [syncthing](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=syncthing)包

- **[Synkron](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Synkron)** — Application that helps you keep your files and folders always updated. You can easily sync your documents, music or pictures to have their latest versions everywhere.

: [https://synkron.sourceforge.net/](https://synkron.sourceforge.net/) || [synkron](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/synkron/)AUR

- **[Timeshift](../../02_系统管理与内核/文件系统/Timeshift.md)** — A system restore tool for Linux.

: [https://github.com/linuxmint/timeshift](https://github.com/linuxmint/timeshift) || [timeshift](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=timeshift)包

- **Vorta** — Encrypted, deduplicated and compressed backups using Borg as backend.

: [https://vorta.borgbase.com/](https://vorta.borgbase.com/) || [vorta](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=vorta)包

### 比较、差异与合并

另见 [Wikipedia:Comparison of file comparison tools](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Comparison_of_file_comparison_tools)。 想要管理 *pacnew*/*pacsave* 文件，有专门的工具。参见 [Pacman/pacnew 与 pacsave#管理 .pac\* 文件](../../01_安装与包管理/包管理器/Pacman_pacnew%20与%20pacsave.md#管理_.pac*_文件)。

#### 控制台

参见 [diff(1)](https://man.archlinux.org/man/diff.1) 来自 [diffutils](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=diffutils)包 和[它的替代品](../../07_开发与工具/命令行/核心工具.md#diff_替代品)。

- **colordiff** — A Perl script wrapper for 'diff' that produces the same output but with pretty 'syntax' highlighting.

: [https://www.colordiff.org/](https://www.colordiff.org/) || [colordiff](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=colordiff)包

- **delta** — A diff viewer written in Rust with syntax highlighting.

: [https://github.com/dandavison/delta](https://github.com/dandavison/delta) || [git-delta](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=git-delta)包

- **diff-highlight** — Perl script to highlight changed parts within pairable lines of `diff -u` output

: [https://github.com/git/git/tree/master/contrib/diff-highlight](https://github.com/git/git/tree/master/contrib/diff-highlight) || [git](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=git)包

- **diffr** — A Rust utility to pretty-print diff with highlighting at word-level for ease of review.

: [https://github.com/mookid/diffr](https://github.com/mookid/diffr) || [diffr](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/diffr/)AUR

- **diff-so-fancy** — A diff output decorator. It does not calculate the diff, it only decorates it.

: [https://github.com/so-fancy/diff-so-fancy](https://github.com/so-fancy/diff-so-fancy) || [diff-so-fancy](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=diff-so-fancy)包

- **difftastic** — Compares files using their syntax trees

: [https://difftastic.wilfred.me.uk](https://difftastic.wilfred.me.uk) || [difftastic](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=difftastic)包

- **riff** — A syntax highlighter for diffs, clarifying which parts of lines have changed.

: [https://github.com/walles/riff](https://github.com/walles/riff) || [riffdiff](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/riffdiff/)AUR

- **Text-Diff** — Perl library that perform diffs on files and record sets.

: [https://metacpan.org/pod/Text::Diff](https://metacpan.org/pod/Text::Diff) || [perl-text-diff](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=perl-text-diff)包

- **ydiff** — A Python wrapper to get highlighted diff output from stdin or VCS-tracked file/dirs, in either unified or side-by-side view.

: [https://github.com/ymattw/ydiff](https://github.com/ymattw/ydiff) || [ydiff](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/ydiff/)AUR

#### 图形界面

- **DiffPDF** — Compare the text or the visual appearance of each page in two PDF files.

: [https://gitlab.com/eang/diffpdf](https://gitlab.com/eang/diffpdf) || [diffpdf](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=diffpdf)包

- **Diffuse** — Small and simple text merge tool written in Python.

: [https://diffuse.sourceforge.net/](https://diffuse.sourceforge.net/) || [diffuse](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=diffuse)包

- **KDiff3** — File and directory diff and merge tool for the KDE desktop.

: [https://apps.kde.org/kdiff3/](https://apps.kde.org/kdiff3/) || [kdiff3](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=kdiff3)包

- **Kompare** — GUI front-end program for viewing and merging differences between source files. It supports a variety of diff formats and provides many options to customize the information level displayed. Part of [kde-sdk](https://archlinux.org/groups/x86_64/kde-sdk/)包组.

: [https://apps.kde.org/kompare/](https://apps.kde.org/kompare/) || [kompare](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=kompare)包

- **[Meld](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Meld_(software))** — Visual diff and merge tool that can compare files, directories, and version controlled projects.

: [https://meldmerge.org/](https://meldmerge.org/) || [meld](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=meld)包

- **Text Compare** — Simple text comparison app.

: [https://github.com/josephmawa/TextCompare](https://github.com/josephmawa/TextCompare) || [textcompare](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/textcompare/)AUR

- **xxdiff** — A graphical browser for file and directory differences.

: [https://furius.ca/xxdiff/](https://furius.ca/xxdiff/) || [xxdiff](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/xxdiff/)AUR

[Vim](../../07_开发与工具/文本编辑器/Vim.md) and [Emacs](../../07_开发与工具/Emacs/Emacs.md) provide merge functionality with [vimdiff](../../07_开发与工具/文本编辑器/Vim.md#Merging_files) and `ediff`.

### 文件搜索

本章节列出了基于文件名、文件路径或元数据的文件搜索实用程序。有关全文搜索，请参见下一章节。 另见 [Wikipedia:List of search engines#Desktop search engines](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_search_engines#Desktop_search_engines)。

#### 控制台

参见 [find(1)](https://man.archlinux.org/man/find.1) 来自 [findutils](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=findutils)包 和[它的替代品](../../07_开发与工具/命令行/核心工具.md#find_替代品)。

#### 图形界面

- **Catfish** — Versatile file searching tool by Xfce, can be powered by find, locate and Zeitgeist.

: [https://launchpad.net/catfish-search](https://launchpad.net/catfish-search) || [catfish](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=catfish)包

- **Clapgrep** — One app to search through all your files.

: [https://github.com/luleyleo/clapgrep](https://github.com/luleyleo/clapgrep) || [clapgrep](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=clapgrep)包

- **CoreHunt** — Easy to use fast file searching tool with categorized search and search history. Part of C-Suite.

: [https://gitlab.com/cubocore/coreapps/corehunt](https://gitlab.com/cubocore/coreapps/corehunt) || [corehunt](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/corehunt/)AUR

- **GNOME Search Tool** — GNOME utility to search for files, depends on [GNOME Files](../../05_桌面与图形/GNOME/GNOME_文件.md).

: [https://gitlab.gnome.org/Archive/gnome-search-tool](https://gitlab.gnome.org/Archive/gnome-search-tool) || [gnome-search-tool](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/gnome-search-tool/)AUR

- **KFind** — Search tool for KDE to find files by name, type or content. Has internal search and supports locate. Part of [kde-utilities](https://archlinux.org/groups/x86_64/kde-utilities/)包组.

: [https://apps.kde.org/kfind/](https://apps.kde.org/kfind/) || [kfind](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=kfind)包

- **MATE Search Tool** — MATE utility to search for files.

: [https://github.com/mate-desktop/mate-utils](https://github.com/mate-desktop/mate-utils) || [mate-utils](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=mate-utils)包

: [https://gnome.pages.gitlab.gnome.org/localsearch/](https://gnome.pages.gitlab.gnome.org/localsearch/) || [localsearch](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=localsearch)包

- **MeiliSearch** — Lightning Fast, Ultra Relevant, and Typo-Tolerant Search Engine.

: [https://www.meilisearch.com/](https://www.meilisearch.com/) || [meilisearch](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=meilisearch)包

- **Namazu** — A Full-Text Search Engine

: [http://www.namazu.org/](http://www.namazu.org/) || [namazu](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=namazu)包

- **[Recoll](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Recoll)** — Full text search tool based on Xapian, has CLI and GUI.

: [https://www.recoll.org/](https://www.recoll.org/) || [recoll](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=recoll)包

### 版本控制系统

另见 [Wikipedia:Comparison of revision control software](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Comparison_of_revision_control_software)。

- **[Breezy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Breezy_(software))** — A decentralized revision control system with support for Bazaar and Git file formats.

: [https://www.breezy-vcs.org/](https://www.breezy-vcs.org/) || [breezy](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=breezy)包

- **CVS** — Concurrent Versions System, a client-server revision control system.

: [https://cvs.nongnu.org/](https://cvs.nongnu.org/) || [cvs](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=cvs)包

- **[Darcs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Darcs)** — Distributed revision control system that was designed to replace traditional, centralized source control systems such as CVS and Subversion.

: [https://darcs.net/](https://darcs.net/) || [darcs](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=darcs)包

- **[Fossil](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fossil_(software))** — Distributed VCS with bug tracking, wiki, forum, and technotes.

: [https://www.fossil-scm.org/](https://www.fossil-scm.org/) || [fossil](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=fossil)包

- **[Git](../../07_开发与工具/版本控制系统/Git.md)** — Distributed revision control and source code management system with an emphasis on speed.

: [https://git-scm.com/](https://git-scm.com/) || [git](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=git)包

- **Got** — A version control system which prioritizes ease of use and simplicity over flexibility.

: [https://gameoftrees.org/](https://gameoftrees.org/) || [gameoftrees](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/gameoftrees/)AUR

- **Jujutsu** — Modern VCS designed for ease of use, offering powerful features while maintaining full Git compatibility.

: [https://docs.jj-vcs.dev/](https://docs.jj-vcs.dev/) || [jujutsu](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=jujutsu)包

- **Mercurial** — Distributed version control system written in Python and similar in many ways to Git.

: [https://www.mercurial-scm.org/](https://www.mercurial-scm.org/) || [mercurial](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=mercurial)包

- **Pijul** — Distributed version control system based on a theory of patches.

: [https://pijul.org/](https://pijul.org/) || [pijul](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/pijul/)AUR

- **Sapling** — A user-friendly, scalable and Git-compatible source control system.

: [https://sapling-scm.com/](https://sapling-scm.com/) || [sapling-scm-bin](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/sapling-scm-bin/)AUR

- **[Subversion](../../07_开发与工具/版本控制系统/Subversion.md)** — Full-featured centralized version control system originally designed to be a better CVS.

: [https://subversion.apache.org/](https://subversion.apache.org/) || [subversion](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=subversion)包

: [https://turbowarp.org](https://turbowarp.org) || [turbowarp-desktop-bin](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/turbowarp-desktop-bin/)AUR

### 调试工具

另见特定编程语言的工具（如 [C#Useful tools](../../07_开发与工具/编程语言/C.md#Useful_tools)、[Java#Development tools](../Oracle/Java.md#Development_tools)）和特定技术的工具（如 [D-Bus#Debugging](../../02_系统管理与内核/系统管理/D-Bus.md#Debugging)）。有关与 IDE／文本编辑器的集成，参见调试适配器协议（DAP）。

- **Accerciser** — Interactive Python accessibility explorer. It uses the AT-SPI library to inspect, examine, and interact with widgets, allowing you to check if an application is providing correct information to assistive technologies and automated testing frameworks. Part of [gnome-extra](https://archlinux.org/groups/x86_64/gnome-extra/)包组.

: [https://wiki.gnome.org/Apps/Accerciser](https://wiki.gnome.org/Apps/Accerciser) || [accerciser](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=accerciser)包

- **Accessibility Inspector** — Inspector for application accessibility tree. Part of [kde-accessibility](https://archlinux.org/groups/x86_64/kde-accessibility/)包组.

: [https://apps.kde.org/accessibilityinspector/](https://apps.kde.org/accessibilityinspector/) || [accessibility-inspector](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=accessibility-inspector)包

- **bin-cpuflags-x86** — A small CLI tool to detect CPU flags (instruction sets) of X86 binaries.

: [https://github.com/HanabishiRecca/bin-cpuflags-x86](https://github.com/HanabishiRecca/bin-cpuflags-x86) || [bin-cpuflags-x86](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/bin-cpuflags-x86/)AUR

- **[Data Display Debugger](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Data_Display_Debugger)** — Graphical front-end for command-line debuggers such as GDB.

: [https://www.gnu.org/software/ddd/](https://www.gnu.org/software/ddd/) || [ddd](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/ddd/)AUR

- **Desed** — TUI-based debugger for sed. Features variable and regex preview, setting breakpoints and stepping back and forth.

- **DrKonqi** — Crash handler for KDE software. It also provides a GUI for `coredumpctl`, called `drkonqi-coredump-gui`. Part of [plasma](https://archlinux.org/groups/x86_64/plasma/)包组.

: [https://invent.kde.org/plasma/drkonqi](https://invent.kde.org/plasma/drkonqi) || [drkonqi](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=drkonqi)包

- **Frida** — Toolkit for inspecting and intercepting function calls.

- **GammaRay** — Qt-application inspection and manipulation tool.

- **GDB** — Native code debugger from the [GNU](../GNU/GNU.md) Project.

- **GDBFrontend** — Easy, flexible and extensible graphical debugger.

### 性能分析与追踪记录工具

有关背景知识，参见 Debugging/Profiling。

- **[gprof](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/gprof)** — Profile analysis tool for programs instrumented with [GCC](../GNU/GNU%20Compiler%20Collection.md).

: [https://sourceware.org/binutils/docs/gprof/](https://sourceware.org/binutils/docs/gprof/) || [binutils](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=binutils)包

- **Heaptrack** — A heap memory profiler for Linux.

: [https://apps.kde.org/heaptrack/](https://apps.kde.org/heaptrack/) || [heaptrack](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=heaptrack)包

- **Hotspot** — GUI for exploring profile data collected with [perf](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=perf)包.

: [https://github.com/KDAB/hotspot](https://github.com/KDAB/hotspot) || [hotspot](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/hotspot/)AUR

- **KCachegrind** — GUI for exploring profile data collected with Valgrind's cachegrind tool. Part of [kde-sdk](https://archlinux.org/groups/x86_64/kde-sdk/)包组.

: [https://apps.kde.org/kcachegrind/](https://apps.kde.org/kcachegrind/) || KDE: [kcachegrind](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=kcachegrind)包, Qt: [qcachegrind](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=qcachegrind)包

- **Massif-Visualizer** — Visualizer for Valgrind Massif data files.

: [https://apps.kde.org/massif_visualizer/](https://apps.kde.org/massif_visualizer/) || [massif-visualizer](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=massif-visualizer)包

- **perf** — Official CLI for the Linux kernel's *perf_events* profiling subsystem.

: [https://perfwiki.github.io/](https://perfwiki.github.io/) || [perf](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=perf)包

- **Sysprof** — Profiling tool that helps in finding the functions in which a program uses most of its time. Part of [gnome-extra](https://archlinux.org/groups/x86_64/gnome-extra/)包组.

: [https://apps.gnome.org/Sysprof/](https://apps.gnome.org/Sysprof/) || [sysprof](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=sysprof)包

> **提示：**Valgrind 的某些内置工具（如 [Cachegrind](https://valgrind.org/docs/manual/cg-manual.html) 和 [Callgrind](https://valgrind.org/docs/manual/cl-manual.html)）也提供性能分析功能。

- **asdf** — Extendable version manager with support for Ruby, Node.js, Elixir, Erlang & more.

: [https://asdf-vm.com](https://asdf-vm.com) || [asdf-vm](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/asdf-vm/)AUR

- **[Nix](../../01_安装与包管理/函数式包管理器/Nix.md)** — A purely functional package manager that aims to make package management reproducible, declarative and reliable.

- **[Guix](../GNU/GNU%20Guix.md)** — A package manager that offers transactional, reproducible, per-user package management.

: [https://guix.gnu.org/](https://guix.gnu.org/) || [guix](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/guix/)AUR

### 词法分析与解析

[Lex](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lex_(software)) and [Yacc](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yacc) are part of POSIX.

- **[Berkeley Yacc](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Berkeley_Yacc)** — Berkeley reimplementation of the Unix parser generator Yacc.

: [https://invisible-island.net/byacc/](https://invisible-island.net/byacc/) || [byacc](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=byacc)包

- **[flex](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Flex_(lexical_analyser_generator))** — A tool for generating text-scanning programs, alternative to Lex.

: [https://github.com/westes/flex](https://github.com/westes/flex) || [flex](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=flex)包

- **[GNU Bison](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/GNU_bison)** — The GNU general-purpose parser generator, alternative to *byacc*.

: [https://www.gnu.org/software/bison/](https://www.gnu.org/software/bison/) || [bison](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=bison)包

然后还有：

- **[ANTLR](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ANTLR)** — Parser generator, written in Java, for parsing structured text or binary files.

: [https://www.antlr.org/](https://www.antlr.org/) || [antlr4](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=antlr4)包

- **LPeg** — Pattern-matching library, based on PEGs, for Lua.

: [https://www.inf.puc-rio.br/~roberto/lpeg/](https://www.inf.puc-rio.br/~roberto/lpeg/) || [lua-lpeg](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=lua-lpeg)包, [lua52-lpeg](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=lua52-lpeg)包, [lua51-lpeg](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=lua51-lpeg)包

- **peg/leg** — Recursive-descent parser generators for C.

: [https://www.piumarta.com/software/peg/](https://www.piumarta.com/software/peg/) || [peg](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=peg)包

- **Ragel** — Compiles finite state machines from regular languages into executable C, C++, Objective-C, or D code.

: [https://www.colm.net/open-source/ragel/](https://www.colm.net/open-source/ragel/) || [ragel](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=ragel)包

- **hexd** — Modern colored hex dump.

- **huxdemp** — Advanced and colored version of hexdump using a pager. Binary name is `huxd`.

- **Graphical Disk Map** — Disk usage analyzer that draws a map of rectangles sized according to file or dir sizes.

### 系统信息查看器

#### 控制台

- **agesafetch** — A tool for identifying the [AGESA](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/AGESA) version embedded in your AMD system's [UEFI](../../01_安装与包管理/启动过程/UEFI.md) firmware.

### 图像处理

#### 终端

- **[G'MIC](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/G%27MIC)** — 功能全面的开源图像处理框架，提供多种用户界面，用于转换、处理、滤镜和可视化各种图像数据集，涵盖从一维标量信号、二维彩色图像到三维空间加时间（3D+t）的多光谱体积图像序列。

: [https://www.gmic.eu/](https://www.gmic.eu/) || [gmic](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=gmic)包

- **[GraphicsMagick](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/GraphicsMagick)** — ImageMagick 的分支，旨在保持 API 和命令行接口的稳定性。还支持多 CPU 并行处理以提高性能，因此被 Flickr、Etsy 等大型商业网站采用。

: [http://www.graphicsmagick.org/](http://www.graphicsmagick.org/) || [graphicsmagick](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=graphicsmagick)包

- **[ImageMagick](../多媒体/ImageMagick.md)** — 命令行图像处理程序，以精确的格式转换能力著称，支持超过 100 种图像格式。其 API 支持脚本化操作，通常用作后端图像处理工具。

- **waifu2x** — 使用深度卷积神经网络对动漫风格图像进行超分辨率放大。

## 音频

### 音频系统

另见主条目 [Sound system](../../04_硬件与外设/音频/音频系统.md) 和 [Wikipedia:Sound server](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sound_server)。

### 音频播放器

另见 [Wikipedia:Comparison of audio player software](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Comparison_of_audio_player_software)。

在 [#视频播放器](#视频播放器)章节的许多应用程序也支持音频播放。

#### 服务器

- **Funkwhale** — self-hosted, modern, free and open-source music server, heavily inspired by Grooveshark. Instances can be federated.

: [https://funkwhale.audio/](https://funkwhale.audio/) || [funkwhale](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/funkwhale/)AUR

- **[Music Player Daemon](../音乐/MPD.md)** — 采用服务器-客户端架构的音频播放器。需要一个单独的客户端才能连接它。

: [https://www.musicpd.org/](https://www.musicpd.org/) || [mpd](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=mpd)包

- **[upmpdcli](https://www.lesbonscomptes.com/upmpdcli/)** — UPnP Media Renderer front-end for Music Player Daemon that interacts with internal streaming services like Gerbera or external streaming services including Internet radio, Tidal, Qobuz, Subsonic; through a control point like [upplay](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/upplay/)AUR and various mobile apps like Linn Kazoo.

: [https://www.lesbonscomptes.com/upmpdcli/](https://www.lesbonscomptes.com/upmpdcli/) || [upmpdcli](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=upmpdcli)包

#### 控制台

- **[Avidemux](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Avidemux)** — Free video editor designed for simple cutting, filtering and encoding tasks.

: [http://fixounet.free.fr/avidemux/](http://fixounet.free.fr/avidemux/) || [avidemux-qt](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=avidemux-qt)包

- **[Blender](../多媒体/Blender.md)** — Fully integrated 3D graphics creation suite with a built-in non-linear video editor.

: [https://www.blender.org/](https://www.blender.org/) || [blender](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=blender)包

- **[Cinelerra (Community Version)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cinelerra)** — Advanced video editing and compositing environment.

: [http://cinelerra-cv.wikidot.com/](http://cinelerra-cv.wikidot.com/) || [cinelerra-cv-git](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/cinelerra-cv-git/)AUR

- **[Cinelerra GG Infinity](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cinelerra)** — Professional video editing and compositing environment.

: [https://www.cinelerra-gg.org/](https://www.cinelerra-gg.org/) || [cinelerra-gg-git](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/cinelerra-gg-git/)AUR

- **[DaVinci Resolve](../视频/DaVinci%20Resolve.md)** — Proprietary A/V post-production software suite.

: [https://www.blackmagicdesign.com/products/davinciresolve/](https://www.blackmagicdesign.com/products/davinciresolve/) || [davinci-resolve](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/davinci-resolve/)AUR

- **[Flowblade](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Flowblade)** — Multitrack non-linear video editor for Linux, designed to provide a fast, robust editing experience.

: [https://jliljebl.github.io/flowblade/](https://jliljebl.github.io/flowblade/) || [flowblade](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=flowblade)包

- **Footage** — Trim, flip, rotate and crop individual clips. Footage is a useful tool for quickly editing short videos and screencasts.

: [https://gitlab.com/adhami3310/Footage](https://gitlab.com/adhami3310/Footage) || [footage](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/footage/)AUR

- **[Kdenlive](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kdenlive)** — Non-linear video editor designed for basic to semi-professional work. Part of [kde-multimedia](https://archlinux.org/groups/x86_64/kde-multimedia/)包组.

: [https://kdenlive.org/](https://kdenlive.org/) || [kdenlive](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=kdenlive)包

- **[Lightworks](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lightworks)** — Professional proprietary non-linear editing system for editing and mastering digital video in various formats.

- **[LiVES](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/LiVES)** — Video editor and VJ (live performance) platform.

: [http://lives-video.com/](http://lives-video.com/) || [lives](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/lives/)AUR

- **LosslessCut** — GUI tool for lossless trimming/cutting of video/audio files. Based on the [Electron](../../07_开发与工具/框架/Electron.md) platform.

: [https://mifi.no/losslesscut/](https://mifi.no/losslesscut/) || [losslesscut-bin](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/losslesscut-bin/)AUR

- **[Natron](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Natron_(software))** — Open-source compositing software. Node-graph based. Similar in functionalities to Adobe After Effects and Nuke by The Foundry.

: [https://natrongithub.github.io/](https://natrongithub.github.io/) || [natron](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/natron/)AUR

- **Olive** — Non-linear video editor aiming to provide a fully-featured alternative to high-end professional video editing software.

- **[Pitivi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pitivi)** — Video editor designed to be intuitive and integrate well in the GNOME desktop.

: [https://www.pitivi.org/](https://www.pitivi.org/) || [pitivi](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=pitivi)包

- **[Shotcut](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shotcut)** — Shotcut is a free, open source, cross-platform video editor.

: [https://www.shotcut.org/](https://www.shotcut.org/) || [shotcut](https://archlinux.org/packages/?name=shotcut)包

- **V2Mixer** — free cross-platform movie editor,mixer

### 无线显示

[Miracast](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Miracast) 是一种无线连接标准，主要使用 [Wi-Fi Direct](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wi-Fi_Direct) 技术实现发送设备与显示接收设备之间的连接。使用 `iw dev` 检查硬件支持情况。使用 `iw reg get` 确保无线电监管域未设置为“Global”，因为这可能会限制某些功能。

- **GNOME Network Displays** — GTK application to cast your desktop to a remote display. Not specific to the [GNOME](../../05_桌面与图形/GNOME/GNOME.md) environment, depends on [PipeWire](../多媒体/PipeWire.md).

: [https://gitlab.gnome.org/GNOME/gnome-network-displays](https://gitlab.gnome.org/GNOME/gnome-network-displays) || [gnome-network-displays](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/gnome-network-displays/)AUR

- **FluxCast** — Lightweight system tray and CLI tool to cast a screen wirelessly via Miracast/WFD, DLNA, or Cast.

: [https://github.com/IlyaP358/fluxcast](https://github.com/IlyaP358/fluxcast) || [fluxcast](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/fluxcast/)AUR

- **MiracleCast** — Command line tools to connect external monitors to your system via Wi-Fi Display specification also known as Miracast.

: [https://github.com/albfan/miraclecast](https://github.com/albfan/miraclecast) || [miraclecast-git](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/miraclecast-git/)AUR

## 元数据

- **CoreInfo** — Simple media metadata viewer built on mediainfo library. Part of C-Suite.

: [https://cubocore.gitlab.io/](https://cubocore.gitlab.io/) || [coreinfo](https://aur.archlinux.org/packages/coreinfo/)AUR

- **DV Analyzer** — Technical quality control and reporting tool that examines DV streams in order to report errors.
